## Comprehensive Architecture Summary

I've thoroughly explored Changelogue and documented the architecture for sources, releases, subscriptions, and the version filtering feature. Here's what I found:

### 1. **Source Model** 
**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/source.go`

```go
type Source struct {
    ID                  string          // UUID
    ProjectID           string          // Foreign key to projects
    Provider            string          // "dockerhub", "github", etc.
    Repository          string          // e.g., "myorg/myrepo"
    PollIntervalSeconds int             // How often to fetch (default 86400)
    Enabled             bool            // Enable/disable polling
    Config              json.RawMessage // Provider-specific config
    LastPolledAt        *time.Time
    LastError           *string
    CreatedAt           time.Time
    UpdatedAt           time.Time
}
```

**Key points:**
- Sources are the **data ingestion endpoints** (Docker Hub, GitHub)
- Each source belongs to a project and is uniquely identified by (provider, repository) pair
- Polling is orchestrated by the `Orchestrator` which loads enabled sources and calls `FetchNewReleases()` on each

---

### 2. **Release Model**
**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/release.go`

```go
type Release struct {
    ID         string          // UUID
    SourceID   string          // Foreign key to sources
    Version    string          // e.g., "v1.2.3", "latest"
    RawData    json.RawMessage // Full metadata from upstream
    ReleasedAt *time.Time
    CreatedAt  time.Time
    
    // Enriched fields from JOINs
    ProjectID   string // Populated from source
    ProjectName string
    Provider    string
    Repository  string
}
```

**Key points:**
- Releases are **immutable raw data** fetched from sources
- Unique constraint: `(source_id, version)` — prevents duplicates
- Created via `service.ProcessResults()` which is called during polling
- Triggers PostgreSQL `NOTIFY` event for real-time SSE updates

---

### 3. **Subscription Model**
**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/subscription.go`

```go
type Subscription struct {
    ID            string    // UUID
    ChannelID     string    // Foreign key to notification_channels
    Type          string    // "source_release" or "semantic_release"
    SourceID      *string   // For source_release subscriptions
    ProjectID     *string   // For semantic_release subscriptions
    VersionFilter string    // REGEX pattern for filtering (optional)
    CreatedAt     time.Time
}
```

**Key points:**
- **Two subscription types:**
  - `source_release`: Subscribe to raw releases from a specific source (has SourceID, no ProjectID)
  - `semantic_release`: Subscribe to AI-analyzed releases for a project (has ProjectID, no SourceID)
- **VersionFilter field:** Already exists in the database schema and API, but NOT USED in notification filtering logic yet
- Database constraint ensures exactly one of (source_id, project_id) is set

---

### 4. **Database Schema**
**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/db/migrations.go` (lines 92-105)

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subscriptions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    channel_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES notification_channels(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('source_release', 'semantic_release')),
    source_id UUID REFERENCES sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    project_id UUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    version_filter TEXT,  -- THIS FIELD EXISTS BUT IS UNUSED
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    CHECK (
        (type = 'source_release'   AND source_id  IS NOT NULL AND project_id IS NULL) OR
        (type = 'semantic_release' AND project_id IS NOT NULL AND source_id  IS NULL)
    )
);
```

---

### 5. **Ingestion Flow**
**Files:** 
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/orchestrator.go` (lines 49-78)
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/service.go` (lines 20-33)
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/source.go` (interface definition)
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/dockerhub.go` (example provider)

**Flow:**
1. `Orchestrator.Run()` polls on a fixed interval
2. Loads all enabled sources via `SourceLoader.LoadEnabledSources()`
3. For each source, calls `FetchNewReleases()` (e.g., `DockerHubSource.FetchNewReleases()`)
4. Returns `[]IngestionResult` with RawVersion, Changelog, Metadata
5. `Service.ProcessResults()` persists via `store.IngestRelease()`
6. Duplicate releases (unique constraint violation) are logged and skipped
7. PostgreSQL trigger fires `NOTIFY 'release_events'` for SSE

---

### 6. **API Endpoints**
**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/subscriptions.go`

**Subscription Endpoints:**
- `GET /subscriptions` — List paginated subscriptions
- `POST /subscriptions` — Create single subscription
- `GET /subscriptions/{id}` — Get subscription details
- `PUT /subscriptions/{id}` — Update subscription (including version_filter)
- `DELETE /subscriptions/{id}` — Delete subscription
- `POST /subscriptions/batch` — Batch create (accepts `version_filter`)
- `DELETE /subscriptions/batch` — Batch delete

**Validation:**
- Type must be `source_release` or `semantic_release`
- channel_id is required
- For `source_release`: source_id required
- For `semantic_release`: project_id required
- version_filter is optional on all endpoints

**TypeScript types** (`web/lib/api/types.ts` lines 153-177):
```typescript
export interface Subscription {
  id: string;
  channel_id: string;
  type: "source_release" | "semantic_release";
  source_id?: string;
  project_id?: string;
  version_filter?: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface BatchSubscriptionInput {
  channel_id: string;
  type: "source_release" | "semantic_release";
  project_ids?: string[];
  source_ids?: string[];
  version_filter?: string;
}
```

---

### 7. **Notification Routing (Worker)**
**Files:**
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go` (lines 58-97)
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/rules.go` (version pattern matching)

**Current Flow:**
1. **Event trigger:** New release inserted → PostgreSQL NOTIFY → enqueues `NotifyJobArgs`
2. **NotifyWorker.Work():**
   - Fetches the release
   - Queries `ListSourceSubscriptions(sourceID)` to find all subscriptions
   - For each subscription:
     - Gets the channel
     - Resolves the appropriate sender (webhook, Slack, Discord)
     - Sends notification (title, body with raw_data)
3. **Agent rules check:**
   - Evaluates project's agent rules (OnMajorRelease, OnMinorRelease, OnSecurityPatch, VersionPattern)
   - Auto-enqueues agent run if criteria match

**⚠️ IMPORTANT:** `version_filter` field is **NOT checked in worker.go**. Notifications are sent to all subscriptions regardless of version_filter value.

---

### 8. **Frontend Components**
**Files:**
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/subscriptions/subscription-form.tsx` (lines 272-286)
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/subscriptions/page.tsx` (lines 191, 284)

**Form Field:**
```tsx
<div className="space-y-2">
  <Label htmlFor="version_filter">Version Filter (regex, optional)</Label>
  <Input 
    id="version_filter" 
    value={versionFilter} 
    onChange={(e) => setVersionFilter(e.target.value)} 
    placeholder='e.g. ^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$' 
  />
</div>
```

**Display in table (page.tsx line 191):**
- Column headers include "Version Filter"
- Shows the filter value for each subscription

---

### 9. **Database Store Implementation**
**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go`

**Subscription methods (lines 310-412):**
- `ListSubscriptions()` — Selects with `COALESCE(version_filter,'')`
- `CreateSubscription()` — Inserts version_filter
- `CreateSubscriptionBatch()` — Transactional insert with version_filter
- `GetSubscription()` — Retrieves with version_filter
- `UpdateSubscription()` — Updates version_filter
- `DeleteSubscription()`
- `DeleteSubscriptionBatch()`

All methods properly persist and retrieve the `version_filter` field.

---

### 10. **Data Flow Summary**

```
Source (Provider)
    ↓
Orchestrator polls FetchNewReleases()
    ↓
IngestionResult objects
    ↓
Service.ProcessResults() → IngestRelease()
    ↓
Release row inserted (unique constraint on source_id + version)
    ↓
PostgreSQL NOTIFY trigger
    ↓
NotifyJobArgs enqueued in River queue
    ↓
NotifyWorker.Work()
    ├─ Fetch Release
    ├─ ListSourceSubscriptions(sourceID)
    └─ For each Subscription:
        ├─ Get Channel
        ├─ Get Sender
        └─ Send Notification to Channel
        
[VERSION FILTER NOT APPLIED HERE — would need to be added]
    ↓
Agent Rules Check (auto-trigger if version pattern matches)
```

---

### **Key Insight on Version Filtering**

The `version_filter` field **exists everywhere** (database, API, TypeScript types, frontend form), but it's **not enforced during notification delivery**. 

In `worker.go` line 58-97:
- The worker fetches all subscriptions for a source
- It sends notifications to **all** subscriptions
- There's **no call** to filter subscriptions based on `version_filter`

This is where version filtering would naturally fit: **In the NotifyWorker, before sending notifications, check if the subscription's version_filter matches the release's version using regex.**

---

    10→// ReleasesStore defines the persistence operations for releases (read-only).
    11→type ReleasesStore interface {
    12→	ListAllReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    13→	ListReleasesBySource(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    14→	ListReleasesByProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    15→	GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error)
    16→}
    17→
    18→// ReleasesHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /releases resource.
    19→type ReleasesHandler struct {
    20→	store ReleasesStore
    21→}
    22→
    23→// NewReleasesHandler returns a new ReleasesHandler.
    24→func NewReleasesHandler(store ReleasesStore) *ReleasesHandler {
    25→	return &ReleasesHandler{store: store}
    26→}
    27→
    28→// List handles GET /releases — returns all releases across all projects.
    29→func (h *ReleasesHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    30→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    31→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListAllReleases(r.Context(), page, perPage)
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    34→		return
    35→	}
    36→	if releases == nil {
    37→		releases = []models.Release{}
    38→	}
    39→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    40→}
    41→
    42→// ListBySource handles GET /sources/{id}/releases — returns releases for a specific source.
    43→func (h *ReleasesHandler) ListBySource(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    44→	sourceID := r.PathValue("id")
    45→	if sourceID == "" {
    46→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Source ID is required")
    47→		return
    48→	}
    49→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    50→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListReleasesBySource(r.Context(), sourceID, page, perPage)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    53→		return
    54→	}
    55→	if releases == nil {
    56→		releases = []models.Release{}
    57→	}
    58→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    59→}
    60→
    61→// ListByProject handles GET /projects/{projectId}/releases — returns releases for all sources in a project.
    62→func (h *ReleasesHandler) ListByProject(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    63→	projectID := r.PathValue("projectId")
    64→	if projectID == "" {
    65→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Project ID is required")
    66→		return
    67→	}
    68→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    69→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListReleasesByProject(r.Context(), projectID, page, perPage)
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    72→		return
    73→	}

    79→
    80→// Get handles GET /releases/{id} — returns a single release.
    81→func (h *ReleasesHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {

   198→func (s *PgStore) ListAllReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {
   199→	var total int
   200→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases`).Scan(&total)
   201→	if err != nil {
   202→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count releases: %w", err)
   203→	}
   204→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   205→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   206→		`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at,
   207→		        COALESCE(p.id,''), COALESCE(p.name,''), COALESCE(s.provider,''), COALESCE(s.repository,'')
   208→		 FROM releases r
   209→		 LEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id
   210→		 LEFT JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id
   211→		 ORDER BY COALESCE(r.released_at, r.created_at) DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list all releases: %w", err)
   214→	}
   215→	defer rows.Close()
   216→	var releases []models.Release
   217→	for rows.Next() {
   218→		var rel models.Release
   219→		if err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt,
   220→			&rel.ProjectID, &rel.ProjectName, &rel.Provider, &rel.Repository); err != nil {
   221→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan release: %w", err)
   222→		}
   223→		releases = append(releases, rel)
   224→	}
   225→	return releases, total, nil
   226→}
   227→

    13→// SourcesStore defines the persistence operations for sources.
    14→type SourcesStore interface {
    15→	ListSourcesByProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Source, int, error)
    16→	CreateSource(ctx context.Context, src *models.Source) error
    17→	GetSource(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Source, error)
    18→	UpdateSource(ctx context.Context, id string, src *models.Source) error
    19→	DeleteSource(ctx context.Context, id string) error
    20→}
    21→
    22→// SourcesHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /sources resource.
    23→type SourcesHandler struct {
    24→	store            SourcesStore
    25→	ingestionService *ingestion.Service
    26→	httpClient       *http.Client
    27→}
    28→
    29→// NewSourcesHandler returns a new SourcesHandler.
    30→func NewSourcesHandler(store SourcesStore, ingestionService *ingestion.Service, httpClient *http.Client) *SourcesHandler {
    31→	return &SourcesHandler{store: store, ingestionService: ingestionService, httpClient: httpClient}
    32→}
    33→
    34→// List handles GET /projects/{projectId}/sources — returns a paginated list of sources for a project.
    35→func (h *SourcesHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    36→	projectID := r.PathValue("projectId")
    37→	if projectID == "" {
    38→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Project ID is required")
    39→		return
    40→	}
    41→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    42→	sources, total, err := h.store.ListSourcesByProject(r.Context(), projectID, page, perPage)
    43→	if err != nil {
    44→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list sources")
    45→		return
    46→	}
    47→	if sources == nil {
    48→		sources = []models.Source{}
    49→	}
    50→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, sources, page, perPage, total)
    51→}
    52→
    53→// Create handles POST /projects/{projectId}/sources — creates a new source under a project.
    54→func (h *SourcesHandler) Create(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    55→	projectID := r.PathValue("projectId")
    56→	if projectID == "" {
    57→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Project ID is required")
    58→		return
    59→	}
    60→	var src models.Source
    61→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &src); err != nil {
    62→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid JSON body")
    63→		return
    64→	}
    65→	src.ProjectID = projectID
    66→	src.Provider = strings.TrimSpace(src.Provider)
    67→	src.Repository = strings.TrimSpace(src.Repository)
    68→	if src.Provider == "" {
    69→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "provider is required")
    70→		return
    71→	}
    72→	if src.Repository == "" {
    73→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "repository is required")
    74→		return
    75→	}
    76→	if err := h.store.CreateSource(r.Context(), &src); err != nil {
    77→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to create source")
    78→		return
    79→	}
    80→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, src)
    81→}
    82→
    83→// Get handles GET /sources/{id} — returns a single source.
    84→func (h *SourcesHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    85→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    86→	if id == "" {
    87→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid source ID")
    88→		return
    89→	}
    90→	src, err := h.store.GetSource(r.Context(), id)
    91→	if err != nil {
    92→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Source not found")
    93→		return
    94→	}
    95→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, src)
    96→}
    97→
    98→// Update handles PUT /sources/{id} — updates an existing source.
    99→func (h *SourcesHandler) Update(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   100→	id := r.PathValue("id")
   101→	if id == "" {
   102→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid source ID")
   103→		return
   104→	}
   105→	var src models.Source
   106→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &src); err != nil {
   107→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid JSON body")
   108→		return
   109→	}
   110→	src.Provider = strings.TrimSpace(src.Provider)
   111→	src.Repository = strings.TrimSpace(src.Repository)
   112→	if src.Provider == "" {
   113→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "provider is required")
   114→		return
   115→	}
   116→	if src.Repository == "" {
   117→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "repository is required")
   118→		return
   119→	}
   120→	if err := h.store.UpdateSource(r.Context(), id, &src); err != nil {
   121→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Source not found")
   122→		return
   123→	}
   124→	src.ID = id
   125→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, src)
   126→}
   127→
   128→// Delete handles DELETE /sources/{id} — deletes a source.
   129→func (h *SourcesHandler) Delete(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   130→	id := r.PathValue("id")
   131→	if id == "" {
   132→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid source ID")
   133→		return
   134→	}
   135→	if err := h.store.DeleteSource(r.Context(), id); err != nil {
   136→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Source not found")
   137→		return
   138→	}
   139→	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
   140→}
   141→
   142→// FetchReleases handles POST /sources/{id}/releases — polls upstream for new
   143→// releases and ingests them immediately.
   144→func (h *SourcesHandler) FetchReleases(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   145→	id := r.PathValue("id")
   146→	if id == "" {
   147→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid source ID")
   148→		return
   149→	}
   150→	src, err := h.store.GetSource(r.Context(), id)
   151→	if err != nil {
   152→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Source not found")
   153→		return
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	ingestionSrc := ingestion.BuildSource(h.httpClient, src.ID, src.Provider, src.Repository)
   157→	if ingestionSrc == nil {
   158→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "unsupported_provider", "Unsupported provider: "+src.Provider)
   159→		return
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	results, err := ingestionSrc.FetchNewReleases(r.Context())
   163→	if err != nil {
   164→		slog.Error("fetch releases failed", "source", src.ID, "err", err)
   165→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadGateway, "upstream_error", "Failed to fetch releases from upstream")
   166→		return
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	newCount := 0
   170→	if len(results) > 0 && h.ingestionService != nil {
   171→		for _, result := range results {
   172→			if procErr := h.ingestionService.ProcessResults(r.Context(), src.ID, src.Provider, []ingestion.IngestionResult{result}); procErr != nil {
   173→				slog.Error("process release failed", "source", src.ID, "version", result.RawVersion, "err", procErr)
   174→				continue
   175→			}
   176→			newCount++
   177→		}
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, map[string]int{"new_releases": newCount})
   181→}
   182→

> AGENT

Here's what I understand so far:

- Sources already have releases stored in the DB.
- When listing releases for a source (e.g. `GET /sources/{id}/releases`), you want to **filter out** certain version patterns at the database level.
- The `Source` model should store a `version_filter` so that when releases are queried for that source, matching versions are excluded.

Before I propose approaches, one clarification:

> AGENT

Got it — both include and exclude patterns. Now let me propose approaches.

## Approaches

Here are 3 ways to implement version filtering on sources:

### Approach 1: Two columns on `sources` table (Recommended)

Add `version_filter_include TEXT` and `version_filter_exclude TEXT` to the `sources` table. When querying releases for a source, apply PostgreSQL `~` (regex match) in the WHERE clause:
- `AND r.version ~ version_filter_include` (if set)
- `AND r.version !~ version_filter_exclude` (if set)

**Pros:** Simple, fast (DB-level filtering), correct pagination counts, minimal code changes.
**Cons:** Two new columns (but they're optional/nullable).

### Approach 2: Single JSONB field on `sources`

Add a single `version_filter JSONB` column containing `{"include": "pattern", "exclude": "pattern"}`.

**Pros:** Single column, flexible for future extensions.
**Cons:** Slightly more complex query construction, harder to validate.

### Approach 3: Application-level filtering

Store the patterns on the source, but filter in Go code after querying.

**Pros:** No SQL changes to queries.
**Cons:** Breaks pagination (counts wrong), wastes DB bandwidth, slower.

**My recommendation is Approach 1** — two simple TEXT columns. It's the most straightforward, keeps filtering at the DB level (correct pagination), and is easy to understand. Does this work for you?

> AGENT

Thank you — Approach 1 it is. I also see your additional context about the filtering UX. It looks like you want:

1. **Version filter on sources** with include/exclude regex patterns (the core feature we're designing)
2. **Pre-release exclusion** — a convenience toggle
3. **Updated/overridden release exclusion** — suppress re-published versions

And the UX should allow **multiple** filter patterns (not just a single regex), with each pattern marked as either include or exclude.

Let me refine the design based on this.

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"regexp"
     5→	"strconv"
     6→	"strings"
     7→
     8→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// CheckAgentRules evaluates the agent rules against the new and previous version
    12→// strings. Returns true if any rule matches and an agent run should be triggered.
    13→func CheckAgentRules(rules *models.AgentRules, newVersion, previousVersion string) bool {
    14→	if rules == nil {
    15→		return false
    16→	}
    17→
    18→	newMajor, newMinor, _ := parseVersion(newVersion)
    19→	oldMajor, oldMinor, _ := parseVersion(previousVersion)
    20→
    21→	if rules.OnMajorRelease && newMajor > oldMajor {
    22→		return true
    23→	}
    24→	if rules.OnMinorRelease && (newMajor > oldMajor || newMinor > oldMinor) {
    25→		return true
    26→	}
    27→	if rules.OnSecurityPatch && isSecurityPatch(newVersion) {
    28→		return true
    29→	}
    30→	if rules.VersionPattern != "" {
    31→		if matched, _ := regexp.MatchString(rules.VersionPattern, newVersion); matched {
    32→			return true
    33→		}
    34→	}
    35→	return false
    36→}
    37→
    38→// parseVersion extracts major, minor, patch integers from a version string.
    39→// It tolerates a leading "v" prefix and pre-release suffixes (e.g. "-security").
    40→func parseVersion(v string) (major, minor, patch int) {
    41→	v = strings.TrimPrefix(v, "v")
    42→	parts := strings.SplitN(v, ".", 3)
    43→	if len(parts) >= 1 {
    44→		major, _ = strconv.Atoi(parts[0])
    45→	}
    46→	if len(parts) >= 2 {
    47→		minor, _ = strconv.Atoi(parts[1])
    48→	}
    49→	if len(parts) >= 3 {
    50→		// Strip pre-release suffix (e.g. "4-security" -> "4")
    51→		patchStr := strings.SplitN(parts[2], "-", 2)[0]
    52→		patch, _ = strconv.Atoi(patchStr)
    53→	}
    54→	return
    55→}
    56→
    57→// isSecurityPatch returns true if the version string contains a known security
    58→// keyword in its pre-release or metadata suffix.
    59→func isSecurityPatch(version string) bool {
    60→	lower := strings.ToLower(version)
    61→	keywords := []string{"security", "cve", "vuln"}
    62→	for _, kw := range keywords {
    63→		if strings.Contains(lower, kw) {
    64→			return true
    65→		}
    66→	}
    67→	return false
    68→}
    69→

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"log/slog"
     8→	"net/http"
     9→	"time"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
    12→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    13→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// NotifyStore is the data access interface required by the notification worker.
    17→type NotifyStore interface {
    18→	GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error)
    19→	GetSource(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Source, error)
    20→	ListSourceSubscriptions(ctx context.Context, sourceID string) ([]models.Subscription, error)
    21→	GetChannel(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.NotificationChannel, error)
    22→	GetProject(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Project, error)
    23→	GetPreviousRelease(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, beforeVersion string) (*models.Release, error)
    24→	EnqueueAgentRun(ctx context.Context, projectID, trigger, version string) error
    25→}
    26→
    27→// NotifyWorker is a River worker that processes NotifyJobArgs.
    28→// It looks up the release, finds all source-level subscriptions, resolves
    29→// the notification channel for each, and dispatches via the appropriate sender.
    30→// After sending notifications, it checks the project's agent rules and
    31→// auto-triggers an agent run if the version criteria are met.
    32→type NotifyWorker struct {
    33→	river.WorkerDefaults[queue.NotifyJobArgs]
    34→	store   NotifyStore
    35→	senders map[string]Sender
    36→}
    37→
    38→// NewSenders returns the default sender map for webhook, Slack, and Discord.
    39→func NewSenders() map[string]Sender {
    40→	return map[string]Sender{
    41→		"webhook": &WebhookSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
    42→		"slack":   &SlackSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
    43→		"discord": &DiscordSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
    44→	}
    45→}
    46→
    47→// NewNotifyWorker creates a NotifyWorker with default senders for webhook, Slack, and Discord.
    48→func NewNotifyWorker(store NotifyStore) *NotifyWorker {
    49→	return &NotifyWorker{
    50→		store:   store,
    51→		senders: NewSenders(),
    52→	}
    53→}
    54→
    55→// Work processes a notify job: fetches the release, resolves subscriptions,
    56→// sends notifications to all matching channels, and checks agent rules to
    57→// auto-trigger agent runs when version criteria are met.
    58→func (w *NotifyWorker) Work(ctx context.Context, job *river.Job[queue.NotifyJobArgs]) error {
    59→	release, err := w.store.GetRelease(ctx, job.Args.ReleaseID)
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return fmt.Errorf("get release: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	subs, err := w.store.ListSourceSubscriptions(ctx, job.Args.SourceID)
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		return fmt.Errorf("list subscriptions: %w", err)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	for _, sub := range subs {
    70→		ch, err := w.store.GetChannel(ctx, sub.ChannelID)
    71→		if err != nil {
    72→			slog.Error("get channel failed", "channel_id", sub.ChannelID, "err", err)
    73→			continue
    74→		}
    75→
    76→		sender, ok := w.senders[ch.Type]
    77→		if !ok {
    78→			slog.Warn("unknown channel type", "type", ch.Type)
    79→			continue
    80→		}
    81→
    82→		msg := Notification{
    83→			Title:   fmt.Sprintf("New release: %s", release.Version),
    84→			Body:    string(release.RawData),
    85→			Version: release.Version,
    86→		}
    87→
    88→		if err := sender.Send(ctx, ch, msg); err != nil {
    89→			slog.Error("send notification failed", "channel", ch.Name, "err", err)
    90→		}
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	// Check agent rules and auto-trigger if criteria are met.
    94→	w.checkAgentRules(ctx, release, job.Args.SourceID)
    95→
    96→	return nil
    97→}
    98→
    99→// checkAgentRules evaluates the project's agent rules against the new release
   100→// and the previous release for this source. If any rule matches, an agent run
   101→// is enqueued. Errors are logged but do not fail the job — notification delivery
   102→// is the primary responsibility.
   103→func (w *NotifyWorker) checkAgentRules(ctx context.Context, release *models.Release, sourceID string) {
   104→	source, err := w.store.GetSource(ctx, sourceID)
   105→	if err != nil {
   106→		slog.Error("get source for agent rules", "source_id", sourceID, "err", err)
   107→		return
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	project, err := w.store.GetProject(ctx, source.ProjectID)
   111→	if err != nil {
   112→		slog.Error("get project for agent rules", "project_id", source.ProjectID, "err", err)
   113→		return
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	// Parse the project's agent_rules JSON.
   117→	var rules models.AgentRules
   118→	if len(project.AgentRules) > 0 {
   119→		if err := json.Unmarshal(project.AgentRules, &rules); err != nil {
   120→			slog.Error("unmarshal agent rules", "project_id", project.ID, "err", err)
   121→			return
   122→		}
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	// Determine the previous version for comparison.
   126→	var previousVersion string
   127→	prev, err := w.store.GetPreviousRelease(ctx, sourceID, release.Version)
   128→	if err != nil {
   129→		slog.Error("get previous release for agent rules", "source_id", sourceID, "err", err)
   130→		return
   131→	}
   132→	if prev != nil {
   133→		previousVersion = prev.Version
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	if CheckAgentRules(&rules, release.Version, previousVersion) {
   137→		trigger := fmt.Sprintf("auto:version:%s", release.Version)
   138→		if err := w.store.EnqueueAgentRun(ctx, source.ProjectID, trigger, release.Version); err != nil {
   139→			slog.Error("enqueue agent run", "project_id", source.ProjectID, "err", err)
   140→		} else {
   141→			slog.Info("agent run triggered by version rules",
   142→				"project_id", source.ProjectID,
   143→				"version", release.Version,
   144→				"previous_version", previousVersion,
   145→			)
   146→		}
   147→	}
   148→}
   149→

     1→package ingestion
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→
     8→	"github.com/google/uuid"
     9→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
    10→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
    11→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
    12→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// PgStore implements ReleaseStore using PostgreSQL + River for the transactional outbox.
    16→type PgStore struct {
    17→	pool  *pgxpool.Pool
    18→	river *river.Client[pgx.Tx]
    19→}
    20→
    21→func NewPgStore(pool *pgxpool.Pool, riverClient *river.Client[pgx.Tx]) *PgStore {
    22→	return &PgStore{pool: pool, river: riverClient}
    23→}
    24→
    25→// IngestRelease inserts a release and enqueues a notify job in a single transaction.
    26→// Returns an error on unique constraint violation (caller treats as idempotent skip).
    27→func (s *PgStore) IngestRelease(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, result *IngestionResult) error {
    28→	tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
    29→	if err != nil {
    30→		return fmt.Errorf("begin tx: %w", err)
    31→	}
    32→	defer tx.Rollback(ctx)
    33→
    34→	// Build raw_data from Metadata + Changelog so nothing is lost.
    35→	raw := make(map[string]string)
    36→	for k, v := range result.Metadata {
    37→		raw[k] = v
    38→	}
    39→	if result.Changelog != "" {
    40→		raw["changelog"] = result.Changelog
    41→	}
    42→	rawData, err := json.Marshal(raw)
    43→	if err != nil {
    44→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal raw_data: %w", err)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	releaseID := uuid.New().String()
    48→	_, err = tx.Exec(ctx,
    49→		`INSERT INTO releases (id, source_id, version, raw_data, released_at) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)`,
    50→		releaseID, sourceID, result.RawVersion, rawData, result.Timestamp,
    51→	)
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		return fmt.Errorf("insert release: %w", err)
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	_, err = s.river.InsertTx(ctx, tx, queue.NotifyJobArgs{
    57→		ReleaseID: releaseID,
    58→		SourceID:  sourceID,
    59→	}, nil)
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return fmt.Errorf("enqueue job: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
    65→		return fmt.Errorf("commit: %w", err)
    66→	}
    67→	return nil
    68→}
    69→

    15→interface SourceFormProps {

    26→export function SourceForm({ initial, onSubmit, title, redirectTo, onSuccess, onCancel }: SourceFormProps) {
    27→  const router = useRouter();
    28→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    29→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    30→  const [provider, setProvider] = useState(initial?.provider ?? "dockerhub");
    31→  const [repository, setRepository] = useState(initial?.repository ?? "");
    32→  const [pollInterval, setPollInterval] = useState(String(initial?.poll_interval_seconds ?? 86400));
    33→  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(initial?.enabled ?? true);
    34→  const [configJson, setConfigJson] = useState(
    35→    JSON.stringify(initial?.config ?? {}, null, 2)
    36→  );

    38→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    39→    e.preventDefault();
    40→    setError("");
    41→
    42→    const repoError = validateRepository(provider, repository);
    43→    if (repoError) {
    44→      setError(repoError);

    47→
    48→    let parsedConfig: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    49→    if (configJson.trim() && configJson.trim() !== "{}") {
    50→      try {
    51→        parsedConfig = JSON.parse(configJson);
    52→      } catch {
    53→        setError("Config must be valid JSON");
    54→        return;
    55→      }
    56→    }
    57→
    58→    setSaving(true);
    59→    try {
    60→      await onSubmit({
    61→        provider,
    62→        repository: repository.trim(),
    63→        poll_interval_seconds: Number(pollInterval),
    64→        enabled,
    65→        config: parsedConfig,
    66→      });
    67→      if (onSuccess) {
    68→        onSuccess();
    69→      } else {
    70→        router.push(redirectTo ?? "/sources");
    71→      }
    72→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    73→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save");
    74→    } finally {
    75→      setSaving(false);
    76→    }
    77→  };
    78→
    79→  const handleCancel = () => {
    80→    if (onCancel) {
    81→      onCancel();
    82→    } else {
    83→      router.back();
    84→    }
    85→  };
    86→
    87→  const formContent = (
    88→    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
    89→      {error && <div className="rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>}
    90→      <div className="space-y-2">
    91→        <Label>Provider</Label>
    92→        <Select value={provider} onValueChange={setProvider}>
    93→          <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
    94→          <SelectContent>
    95→            <SelectItem value="dockerhub">Docker Hub</SelectItem>
    96→            <SelectItem value="github">GitHub</SelectItem>
    97→          </SelectContent>
    98→        </Select>
    99→      </div>
   100→      <div className="space-y-2">
   101→        <Label htmlFor="repository">Repository</Label>
   102→        <Input id="repository" value={repository} onChange={(e) => setRepository(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. library/golang or ethereum/go-ethereum" required />
   103→      </div>
   104→      <div className="space-y-2">
   105→        <Label htmlFor="poll_interval">Poll Interval (seconds)</Label>
   106→        <Input id="poll_interval" type="number" min={60} value={pollInterval} onChange={(e) => setPollInterval(e.target.value)} />
   107→      </div>
   108→      <div className="space-y-2">
   109→        <Label htmlFor="config">Config (JSON, optional)</Label>
   110→        <Textarea
   111→          id="config"
   112→          value={configJson}
   113→          onChange={(e) => setConfigJson(e.target.value)}
   114→          rows={4}
   115→          className="font-mono text-sm"
   116→          placeholder="{}"
   117→        />
   118→      </div>
   119→      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   120→        <Switch checked={enabled} onCheckedChange={setEnabled} />
   121→        <Label>Enabled</Label>
   122→      </div>
   123→      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
   124→        <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={handleCancel}>Cancel</Button>
   125→        <Button type="submit" disabled={saving}>{saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</Button>
   126→      </div>
   127→    </form>
   128→  );
   129→
   130→  if (onSuccess) {
   131→    return formContent;
   132→  }
   133→
   134→  return (
   135→    <Card className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
   136→      <CardHeader><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader>
   137→      <CardContent>
   138→        {formContent}
   139→      </CardContent>
   140→    </Card>
   141→  );
   142→}
   143→

Now I have everything I need. Let me present the design. 

## Design: Version Filter for Source Releases

Here's the full design. I'll present it section by section.

### 1. Data Model — Source Table Changes

Add three new columns to the `sources` table:

| Column | Type | Default | Description |
|--------|------|---------|-------------|
| `version_filter_exclude` | `TEXT` | `NULL` | Regex pattern — versions matching this are hidden |
| `version_filter_include` | `TEXT` | `NULL` | Regex pattern — only versions matching this are shown |
| `exclude_prereleases` | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | Convenience toggle — excludes versions containing `-alpha`, `-beta`, `-rc`, `-dev`, `-pre`, `-snapshot` |

**"Overridden/re-published releases"**: Since `UNIQUE(source_id, version)` already prevents duplicate versions, and the INSERT fails (no upsert), overridden releases are already suppressed at ingestion time. No change needed for this.

**Filter precedence**: Include is applied first (whitelist), then Exclude (blacklist), then pre-release exclusion. All are optional.

### 2. DB Query Changes

The `ListReleasesBySource` and `ListReleasesByProject` queries in `pgstore.go` will JOIN with `sources` and apply conditional WHERE clauses:

```sql
-- Only if source.version_filter_include IS NOT NULL:
AND r.version ~ s.version_filter_include
-- Only if source.version_filter_exclude IS NOT NULL:  
AND r.version !~ s.version_filter_exclude
-- Only if source.exclude_prereleases = true:
AND r.version !~ '-(alpha|beta|rc|dev|pre|snapshot|nightly)'
```

For `ListAllReleases`, the same filters apply since we already JOIN sources.

### 3. Notification Worker Changes

In `worker.go`, after fetching the release and before sending notifications, check the source's filters. If the release version doesn't pass the filters, skip notification entirely (both channel notifications and agent rule checks).

### 4. API Changes

The existing `POST/PUT /sources` endpoints will accept the new fields. The `GET /sources` response will include them.

### 5. Frontend Changes

Add to the source form (`source-form.tsx`):
- A "Version Filters" section with:
  - "Exclude Pre-Releases" toggle (Switch component)
  - "Include Pattern" text input (regex)
  - "Exclude Pattern" text input (regex)

Does this design look right?

Got it — removing `exclude_prereleases`. The design simplifies to:

### Updated Design

**Source table additions:**
- `version_filter_include TEXT` — regex, only show versions matching this (optional)
- `version_filter_exclude TEXT` — regex, hide versions matching this (optional)

No pre-release toggle. Users can achieve the same by setting exclude to `-(alpha|beta|rc|dev|pre|snapshot)` manually.

Everything else stays the same (DB-level filtering in queries, notification worker filtering, API + frontend changes).

     1→# Version Filter for Source Releases
     2→
     3→**Date:** 2026-03-01
     4→**Status:** Approved
     5→
     6→## Problem
     7→
     8→When fetching/viewing releases from a source, users need to filter out irrelevant version patterns (e.g., pre-releases, nightly builds, specific version ranges). Currently all releases are shown and all trigger notifications regardless of relevance.
     9→
    10→## Design
    11→
    12→### Data Model
    13→
    14→Add two nullable TEXT columns to the `sources` table:
    15→
    16→| Column | Type | Default | Description |
    17→|--------|------|---------|-------------|
    18→| `version_filter_include` | `TEXT` | `NULL` | Regex — only versions matching this are shown |
    19→| `version_filter_exclude` | `TEXT` | `NULL` | Regex — versions matching this are hidden |
    20→
    21→**Filter precedence:** Include is applied first (whitelist), then Exclude (blacklist). Both are optional — when NULL, no filtering is applied for that dimension.
    22→
    23→### DB Query Changes
    24→
    25→All release listing queries (`ListAllReleases`, `ListReleasesBySource`, `ListReleasesByProject`) JOIN with `sources` and apply conditional WHERE clauses using PostgreSQL `~` regex operator:
    26→
    27→```sql
    28→-- When source.version_filter_include IS NOT NULL:
    29→AND r.version ~ s.version_filter_include
    30→-- When source.version_filter_exclude IS NOT NULL:
    31→AND r.version !~ s.version_filter_exclude
    32→```
    33→
    34→Both the count query and data query must include these filters for correct pagination.
    35→
    36→### Notification Worker Changes
    37→
    38→In `routing/worker.go`, after fetching the release and its source, check whether the release version passes the source's filters. If it doesn't, skip notification delivery and agent rule checks entirely.
    39→
    40→### API Changes
    41→
    42→Existing `POST/PUT /sources` endpoints accept new fields. `GET` responses include them.
    43→
    44→### Frontend Changes
    45→
    46→Add "Version Filters" section to source form (`source-form.tsx`) with:
    47→- "Include Pattern" text input (regex, optional)
    48→- "Exclude Pattern" text input (regex, optional)
    49→
    50→### Migration
    51→
    52→Add migration step to ALTER the sources table with the two new columns.
    53→
    54→## Examples
    55→
    56→- Exclude pre-releases: set `version_filter_exclude` = `-(alpha|beta|rc|dev|pre|snapshot|nightly)`
    57→- Only semver: set `version_filter_include` = `^v?\d+\.\d+\.\d+$`
    58→- Exclude v1.x: set `version_filter_exclude` = `^v?1\.`

     1→package db
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→
     7→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
     8→	"github.com/riverqueue/river/riverdriver/riverpgxv5"
     9→	"github.com/riverqueue/river/rivermigrate"
    10→)
    11→
    12→const schema = `
    13→-- Tracked software projects (the central entity)
    14→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects (
    15→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    16→    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    17→    description TEXT,
    18→    agent_prompt TEXT,
    19→    agent_rules JSONB,
    20→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    21→    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
    22→);
    23→
    24→-- Configured ingestion sources (polling-based: GitHub, Docker Hub)
    25→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sources (
    26→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    27→    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    28→    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    29→    repository VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    30→    poll_interval_seconds INT DEFAULT 86400,
    31→    enabled BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    32→    config JSONB,
    33→    last_polled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    34→    last_error TEXT,
    35→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    36→    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    37→    UNIQUE(provider, repository)
    38→);
    39→
    40→-- Context sources (read-only references for agent research)
    41→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS context_sources (
    42→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    43→    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    44→    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    45→    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    46→    config JSONB NOT NULL,
    47→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    48→    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
    49→);
    50→
    51→-- Source-level releases (detected from polling sources)
    52→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS releases (
    53→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    54→    source_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    55→    version VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    56→    raw_data JSONB,
    57→    released_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    58→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    59→    UNIQUE(source_id, version)
    60→);
    61→
    62→-- Project-level semantic releases (AI-generated, correlating source releases)
    63→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS semantic_releases (
    64→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    65→    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    66→    version VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    67→    report JSONB,
    68→    status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    69→    error TEXT,
    70→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    71→    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    72→    UNIQUE(project_id, version)
    73→);
    74→
    75→-- Join table: which source releases compose a semantic release
    76→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS semantic_release_sources (
    77→    semantic_release_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES semantic_releases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    78→    release_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES releases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    79→    PRIMARY KEY (semantic_release_id, release_id)
    80→);
    81→
    82→-- Notification channels (standalone)
    83→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notification_channels (
    84→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    85→    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    86→    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    87→    config JSONB NOT NULL,
    88→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    89→    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
    90→);
    91→
    92→-- Subscriptions: two types (source releases and semantic releases)
    93→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subscriptions (
    94→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    95→    channel_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES notification_channels(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    96→    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('source_release', 'semantic_release')),
    97→    source_id UUID REFERENCES sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    98→    project_id UUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    99→    version_filter TEXT,
   100→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
   101→    CHECK (
   102→        (type = 'source_release'   AND source_id  IS NOT NULL AND project_id IS NULL) OR
   103→        (type = 'semantic_release' AND project_id IS NOT NULL AND source_id  IS NULL)
   104→    )
   105→);
   106→
   107→-- Agent runs (scoped to project)
   108→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_runs (
   109→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
   110→    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   111→    semantic_release_id UUID REFERENCES semantic_releases(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
   112→    trigger VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
   113→    status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
   114→    prompt_used TEXT,
   115→    error TEXT,
   116→    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
   117→    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
   118→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
   119→);
   120→
   121→ALTER TABLE agent_runs ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS version VARCHAR(100);
   122→
   123→-- API authentication keys
   124→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_keys (
   125→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
   126→    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
   127→    key_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
   128→    key_prefix VARCHAR(12) NOT NULL,
   129→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
   130→    last_used_at TIMESTAMPTZ
   131→);
   132→
   133→-- Trigger for SSE: notify on new releases
   134→CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION notify_release_created() RETURNS trigger AS $$
   135→BEGIN
   136→    PERFORM pg_notify('release_events', json_build_object('type', 'release', 'id', NEW.id)::text);
   137→    RETURN NEW;
   138→END;
   139→$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
   140→
   141→DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS release_created_trigger ON releases;
   142→CREATE TRIGGER release_created_trigger
   143→    AFTER INSERT ON releases
   144→    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION notify_release_created();
   145→
   146→-- Trigger for SSE: notify on semantic release completion
   147→CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION notify_semantic_release() RETURNS trigger AS $$
   148→BEGIN
   149→    IF NEW.status = 'completed' AND (OLD IS NULL OR OLD.status != 'completed') THEN
   150→        PERFORM pg_notify('release_events', json_build_object('type', 'semantic_release', 'id', NEW.id)::text);
   151→    END IF;
   152→    RETURN NEW;
   153→END;
   154→$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
   155→
   156→DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS semantic_release_trigger ON semantic_releases;
   157→CREATE TRIGGER semantic_release_trigger
   158→    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON semantic_releases
   159→    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION notify_semantic_release();
   160→`
   161→
   162→// RunMigrations applies River's schema and the application schema. Idempotent — safe to call on every startup.
   163→func RunMigrations(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool) error {
   164→	migrator, err := rivermigrate.New(riverpgxv5.New(pool), nil)
   165→	if err != nil {
   166→		return fmt.Errorf("create river migrator: %w", err)
   167→	}
   168→	if _, err := migrator.Migrate(ctx, rivermigrate.DirectionUp, nil); err != nil {
   169→		return fmt.Errorf("river migrations: %w", err)
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, schema); err != nil {
   173→		return fmt.Errorf("app migrations: %w", err)
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	// Migrate existing subscription type values: source→source_release, project→semantic_release.
   177→	// Drop old CHECK constraints first (both the named type check and the unnamed
   178→	// composite check referencing old values), then UPDATE data, then re-add constraints.
   179→	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, `
   180→		ALTER TABLE subscriptions DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS subscriptions_type_check;
   181→		ALTER TABLE subscriptions DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS subscriptions_check;
   182→		UPDATE subscriptions SET type = 'source_release' WHERE type = 'source';
   183→		UPDATE subscriptions SET type = 'semantic_release' WHERE type = 'project';
   184→		ALTER TABLE subscriptions ADD CONSTRAINT subscriptions_type_check CHECK (type IN ('source_release', 'semantic_release'));
   185→		ALTER TABLE subscriptions ADD CONSTRAINT subscriptions_check CHECK (
   186→			(type = 'source_release'   AND source_id IS NOT NULL AND project_id IS NULL) OR
   187→			(type = 'semantic_release' AND project_id IS NOT NULL AND source_id IS NULL)
   188→		);
   189→	`); err != nil {
   190→		return fmt.Errorf("subscription type migration: %w", err)
   191→	}
   192→	return nil
   193→}
   194→

   104→// --- KeyStore ---
   105→
   106→func (s *PgStore) ValidateKey(ctx context.Context, rawKey string) (bool, error) {
   107→	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(rawKey))
   108→	keyHash := hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
   109→	var exists bool
   110→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   111→		`SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM api_keys WHERE key_hash = $1)`, keyHash,
   112→	).Scan(&exists)
   113→	return exists, err
   114→}
   115→
   116→func (s *PgStore) TouchKeyUsage(ctx context.Context, rawKey string) {
   117→	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(rawKey))
   118→	keyHash := hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
   119→	_, _ = s.pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE api_keys SET last_used_at = NOW() WHERE key_hash = $1`, keyHash)
   120→}
   121→
   122→// --- SourcesStore ---
   123→
   124→func (s *PgStore) ListSourcesByProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Source, int, error) {
   125→	var total int
   126→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sources WHERE project_id = $1`, projectID).Scan(&total)
   127→	if err != nil {
   128→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count sources: %w", err)
   129→	}
   130→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   131→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   132→		`SELECT id, project_id, provider, repository, poll_interval_seconds, enabled,
   133→		        COALESCE(config,'{}'), last_polled_at, last_error, created_at, updated_at
   134→		 FROM sources WHERE project_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3`, projectID, perPage, offset)
   135→	if err != nil {
   136→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list sources: %w", err)
   137→	}
   138→	defer rows.Close()
   139→	var sources []models.Source
   140→	for rows.Next() {
   141→		var src models.Source
   142→		if err := rows.Scan(&src.ID, &src.ProjectID, &src.Provider, &src.Repository,
   143→			&src.PollIntervalSeconds, &src.Enabled, &src.Config,
   144→			&src.LastPolledAt, &src.LastError, &src.CreatedAt, &src.UpdatedAt); err != nil {
   145→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan source: %w", err)
   146→		}
   147→		sources = append(sources, src)
   148→	}
   149→	return sources, total, nil
   150→}
   151→
   152→func (s *PgStore) CreateSource(ctx context.Context, src *models.Source) error {
   153→	return s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   154→		`INSERT INTO sources (project_id, provider, repository, poll_interval_seconds, enabled, config)
   155→		 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
   156→		 RETURNING id, enabled, created_at, updated_at`,
   157→		src.ProjectID, src.Provider, src.Repository, src.PollIntervalSeconds, src.Enabled, src.Config,
   158→	).Scan(&src.ID, &src.Enabled, &src.CreatedAt, &src.UpdatedAt)
   159→}

    47→}

    49→func (m *mockNotifyStore) GetSource(_ context.Context, id string) (*models.Source, error) {
    50→	if m.err != nil {
    51→		return nil, m.err
    52→	}
    53→	src, ok := m.sources[id]
    54→	if !ok {
    55→		return nil, errors.New("source not found")
    56→	}
    57→	return src, nil
    58→}
    59→
    60→func (m *mockNotifyStore) ListSourceSubscriptions(_ context.Context, sourceID string) ([]models.Subscription, error) {
    61→	if m.err != nil {
    62→		return nil, m.err
    63→	}
    64→	return m.subscriptions[sourceID], nil
    65→}
    66→
    67→func (m *mockNotifyStore) GetChannel(_ context.Context, id string) (*models.NotificationChannel, error) {
    68→	if m.err != nil {
    69→		return nil, m.err
    70→	}
    71→	ch, ok := m.channels[id]
    72→	if !ok {
    73→		return nil, errors.New("channel not found")
    74→	}
    75→	return ch, nil
    76→}
    77→
    78→func (m *mockNotifyStore) GetProject(_ context.Context, id string) (*models.Project, error) {
    79→	if m.err != nil {
    80→		return nil, m.err
    81→	}
    82→	if m.projects == nil {
    83→		return nil, errors.New("project not found")
    84→	}
    85→	p, ok := m.projects[id]
    86→	if !ok {
    87→		return nil, errors.New("project not found")
    88→	}
    89→	return p, nil
    90→}
    91→
    92→func (m *mockNotifyStore) GetPreviousRelease(_ context.Context, sourceID string, _ string) (*models.Release, error) {
    93→	if m.err != nil {
    94→		return nil, m.err
    95→	}
    96→	if m.previousReleases == nil {
    97→		return nil, nil
    98→	}
    99→	return m.previousReleases[sourceID], nil
   100→}
   101→
   102→func (m *mockNotifyStore) EnqueueAgentRun(_ context.Context, projectID, trigger, version string) error {
   103→	if m.enqueueAgentErr != nil {
   104→		return m.enqueueAgentErr
   105→	}
   106→	m.mu.Lock()
   107→	defer m.mu.Unlock()
   108→	m.agentRunCalls = append(m.agentRunCalls, agentRunCall{ProjectID: projectID, Trigger: trigger, Version: version})
   109→	return nil
   110→}
   111→
   112→func (m *mockNotifyStore) agentRunCallCount() int {
   113→	m.mu.Lock()
   114→	defer m.mu.Unlock()
   115→	return len(m.agentRunCalls)
   116→}
   117→
   118→// --- mock sender ---
   119→
   120→type mockSender struct {
   121→	mu      sync.Mutex
   122→	sent    []Notification
   123→	sendErr error
   124→}
   125→
   126→func (m *mockSender) Send(_ context.Context, _ *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
   127→	if m.sendErr != nil {
   128→		return m.sendErr
   129→	}
   130→	m.mu.Lock()
   131→	defer m.mu.Unlock()
   132→	m.sent = append(m.sent, msg)
   133→	return nil
   134→}
   135→
   136→func (m *mockSender) sentCount() int {
   137→	m.mu.Lock()
   138→	defer m.mu.Unlock()
   139→	return len(m.sent)
   140→}

   144→func TestNotifyWorker_Work(t *testing.T) {
   145→	sourceID := "src-1"
   146→	releaseID := "rel-1"
   147→	channelID := "ch-1"
   148→
   149→	store := &mockNotifyStore{
   150→		releases: map[string]*models.Release{
   151→			releaseID: {ID: releaseID, SourceID: sourceID, Version: "v1.14.0", RawData: json.RawMessage(`{"tag":"v1.14.0"}`)},
   152→		},
   153→		sources: map[string]*models.Source{
   154→			sourceID: {ID: sourceID, ProjectID: "proj-1", Provider: "github", Repository: "ethereum/go-ethereum"},
   155→		},
   156→		subscriptions: map[string][]models.Subscription{
   157→			sourceID: {
   158→				{ID: "sub-1", ChannelID: channelID, Type: "source_release", SourceID: &sourceID},
   159→			},
   160→		},
   161→		channels: map[string]*models.NotificationChannel{
   162→			channelID: {ID: channelID, Name: "test-webhook", Type: "webhook", Config: json.RawMessage(`{"url":"http://example.com"}`)},
   163→		},
   164→		projects: map[string]*models.Project{
   165→			"proj-1": {ID: "proj-1", Name: "test", AgentRules: json.RawMessage(`{}`)},
   166→		},
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	webhookSender := &mockSender{}
   170→	worker := &NotifyWorker{
   171→		store: store,
   172→		senders: map[string]Sender{
   173→			"webhook": webhookSender,
   174→		},
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	job := &river.Job[queue.NotifyJobArgs]{
   178→		Args: queue.NotifyJobArgs{
   179→			ReleaseID: releaseID,
   180→			SourceID:  sourceID,
   181→		},
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	err := worker.Work(context.Background(), job)
   185→	if err != nil {
   186→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   187→	}
   188→	if webhookSender.sentCount() != 1 {
   189→		t.Fatalf("expected 1 notification sent, got %d", webhookSender.sentCount())
   190→	}
   191→	if webhookSender.sent[0].Version != "v1.14.0" {
   192→		t.Errorf("expected version v1.14.0, got %s", webhookSender.sent[0].Version)
   193→	}
   194→}

   382→func TestNotifyWorker_AgentRulesTriggered_MajorBump(t *testing.T) {
   383→	sourceID := "src-1"
   384→	releaseID := "rel-1"
   385→	projectID := "proj-1"
   386→
   387→	store := &mockNotifyStore{
   388→		releases: map[string]*models.Release{
   389→			releaseID: {ID: releaseID, SourceID: sourceID, Version: "v2.0.0", RawData: json.RawMessage(`{}`)},
   390→		},
   391→		sources: map[string]*models.Source{
   392→			sourceID: {ID: sourceID, ProjectID: projectID},
   393→		},
   394→		subscriptions: map[string][]models.Subscription{},
   395→		channels:      map[string]*models.NotificationChannel{},
   396→		projects: map[string]*models.Project{
   397→			projectID: {
   398→				ID:         projectID,
   399→				Name:       "test-project",
   400→				AgentRules: json.RawMessage(`{"on_major_release":true}`),
   401→			},
   402→		},
   403→		previousReleases: map[string]*models.Release{
   404→			sourceID: {ID: "rel-0", SourceID: sourceID, Version: "v1.5.3"},
   405→		},
   406→	}
   407→
   408→	worker := &NotifyWorker{store: store, senders: map[string]Sender{}}
   409→
   410→	job := &river.Job[queue.NotifyJobArgs]{
   411→		Args: queue.NotifyJobArgs{ReleaseID: releaseID, SourceID: sourceID},
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	err := worker.Work(context.Background(), job)
   415→	if err != nil {
   416→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   417→	}
   418→	if store.agentRunCallCount() != 1 {
   419→		t.Fatalf("expected 1 EnqueueAgentRun call, got %d", store.agentRunCallCount())
   420→	}
   421→	if store.agentRunCalls[0].ProjectID != projectID {
   422→		t.Errorf("expected project ID %s, got %s", projectID, store.agentRunCalls[0].ProjectID)
   423→	}
   424→	expectedTrigger := "auto:version:v2.0.0"
   425→	if store.agentRunCalls[0].Trigger != expectedTrigger {
   426→		t.Errorf("expected trigger %q, got %q", expectedTrigger, store.agentRunCalls[0].Trigger)
   427→	}
   428→}

     1→# Version Filter for Source Releases — Implementation Plan
     2→
     3→> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.
     4→
     5→**Goal:** Add `version_filter_include` and `version_filter_exclude` regex fields to sources, applied at the DB level when listing releases and at the worker level when sending notifications.
     6→
     7→**Architecture:** Two nullable TEXT columns on the `sources` table. PostgreSQL `~` / `!~` operators filter releases in SQL queries. The notification worker checks filters before dispatching. Frontend source form gets two new text inputs.
     8→
     9→**Tech Stack:** Go, PostgreSQL (regex operators), Next.js/React, Tailwind CSS

   181→The query already JOINs sources. Add conditional WHERE clauses:
   182→
   183→```go
   184→func (s *PgStore) ListAllReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {
   185→	var total int
   186→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   187→		`SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases r
   188→		 LEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id
   189→		 WHERE (s.version_filter_include IS NULL OR r.version ~ s.version_filter_include)
   190→		   AND (s.version_filter_exclude IS NULL OR r.version !~ s.version_filter_exclude)`).Scan(&total)
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count releases: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   195→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   196→		`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at,
   197→		        COALESCE(p.id,''), COALESCE(p.name,''), COALESCE(s.provider,''), COALESCE(s.repository,'')
   198→		 FROM releases r
   199→		 LEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id
   200→		 LEFT JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id
   201→		 WHERE (s.version_filter_include IS NULL OR r.version ~ s.version_filter_include)
   202→		   AND (s.version_filter_exclude IS NULL OR r.version !~ s.version_filter_exclude)
   203→		 ORDER BY COALESCE(r.released_at, r.created_at) DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)
   204→	if err != nil {
   205→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list all releases: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	defer rows.Close()
   208→	var releases []models.Release
   209→	for rows.Next() {
   210→		var rel models.Release
   211→		if err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt,
   212→			&rel.ProjectID, &rel.ProjectName, &rel.Provider, &rel.Repository); err != nil {
   213→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan release: %w", err)
   214→		}
   215→		releases = append(releases, rel)
   216→	}
   217→	return releases, total, nil
   218→}
   219→```

   455→
   456→```go
   457→// VersionPassesFilter returns true if the version string passes the source's
   458→// include/exclude regex filters. When both are nil the version always passes.
   459→func VersionPassesFilter(version string, include, exclude *string) bool {
   460→	if include != nil && *include != "" {
   461→		matched, err := regexp.MatchString(*include, version)
   462→		if err != nil || !matched {
   463→			return false
   464→		}
   465→	}
   466→	if exclude != nil && *exclude != "" {
   467→		matched, err := regexp.MatchString(*exclude, version)
   468→		if err != nil || matched {
   469→			return false
   470→		}
   471→	}
   472→	return true
   473→}
   474→```

{
  "description": "Update release listing queries",
  "prompt": "You are implementing Task 4 of a version filter feature for Changelogue. This is a Go project at /Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon.\n\n## Context\nThe `sources` table now has `version_filter_include TEXT` and `version_filter_exclude TEXT` columns (nullable). When listing releases, we need to apply these regex filters at the SQL level using PostgreSQL `~` (match) and `!~` (not match) operators.\n\n## What to do\n\nFile: `internal/api/pgstore.go`\n\nUpdate these three functions to apply source version filters in both the COUNT query and the data query:\n\n### 1. `ListAllReleases` (around line 198)\n\nThe count query currently is:\n```sql\nSELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases\n```\n\nChange to:\n```sql\nSELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases r\nLEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id\nWHERE (s.version_filter_include IS NULL OR r.version ~ s.version_filter_include)\n  AND (s.version_filter_exclude IS NULL OR r.version !~ s.version_filter_exclude)\n```\n\nThe data query already has `LEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id`. Just add the same WHERE clause before the ORDER BY:\n```sql\nWHERE (s.version_filter_include IS NULL OR r.version ~ s.version_filter_include)\n  AND (s.version_filter_exclude IS NULL OR r.version !~ s.version_filter_exclude)\n```\n\n### 2. `ListReleasesBySource` (around line 228)\n\nCount query changes from:\n```sql\nSELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases WHERE source_id = $1\n```\nTo:\n```sql\nSELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases r\nJOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id\nWHERE r.source_id = $1\n  AND (s.version_filter_include IS NULL OR r.version ~ s.version_filter_include)\n  AND (s.version_filter_exclude IS NULL OR r.version !~ s.version_filter_exclude)\n```\n\nData query: add the same two AND clauses after `WHERE r.source_id = $1`.\n\n### 3. `ListReleasesByProject` (around line 258)\n\nCount query: add after `WHERE s.project_id = $1`:\n```sql\nAND (s.version_filter_include IS NULL OR r.version ~ s.version_filter_include)\n  AND (s.version_filter_exclude IS NULL OR r.version !~ s.version_filter_exclude)\n```\n\nNote: count query needs `r` alias. Change from:\n```sql\nSELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases r JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id WHERE s.project_id = $1\n```\n\nData query: same two AND clauses before ORDER BY.\n\n## Verification\n- Run: `go vet ./internal/api/...`\n- Run: `go test ./internal/api/... -run TestReleases -v` (if they exist, otherwise just vet)\n\n## Commit\n```\nfeat: apply source version filters when listing releases\n```\n",

All done. Here is a summary of what was changed.

## Summary

**File modified:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go`

Three functions were updated to apply source-level version filters (`version_filter_include` and `version_filter_exclude`) when listing releases. Both the COUNT query and the data query in each function now include the filter conditions using PostgreSQL regex operators (`~` and `!~`). The filters are nullable-safe: when a filter column is NULL, the condition is skipped.

### 1. `ListAllReleases` (line 205)
- **Count query:** Changed from a simple `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases` to a query that joins `sources` and applies the two filter conditions.
- **Data query:** Added a `WHERE` clause with the same two filter conditions before `ORDER BY`.

### 2. `ListReleasesBySource` (line 241)
- **Count query:** Changed from `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases WHERE source_id = $1` to a query that joins `sources` and adds the filter conditions as additional `AND` clauses.
- **Data query:** Added the two filter conditions as `AND` clauses after `WHERE r.source_id = $1`.

### 3. `ListReleasesByProject` (line 279)
- **Count query:** Added the two filter conditions as `AND` clauses after `WHERE s.project_id = $1`.
- **Data query:** Added the two filter conditions as `AND` clauses after `WHERE s.project_id = $1`.

### Filter logic
```sql
AND (s.version_filter_include IS NULL OR r.version ~ s.version_filter_include)
AND (s.version_filter_exclude IS NULL OR r.version !~ s.version_filter_exclude)
```

### Verification
- `go vet ./internal/api/...` -- passed cleanly
- `go test ./internal/api/... -run TestReleases -v` -- all 4 tests pass (TestReleasesHandlerListBySource, TestReleasesHandlerListByProject, TestReleasesHandlerGet, TestReleasesHandlerGetNotFound)
- Committed as `685547a`.

All changes are complete. Here is a summary of what was done:

---

## Changes Made

### `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go`

1. **Added `"regexp"` import** to support regex matching in version filters.

2. **Added `VersionPassesFilter` helper function** (lines 56-72) -- a public function that checks a version string against optional include/exclude regex patterns. It returns `true` if:
   - The include pattern is nil/empty OR the version matches the include pattern
   - The exclude pattern is nil/empty OR the version does NOT match the exclude pattern

3. **Modified `Work` method** -- now fetches the source immediately after the release and calls `VersionPassesFilter` before processing subscriptions. If the version is filtered out, the worker returns early with `nil` (no notifications sent, no agent rules checked).

4. **Updated `checkAgentRules` signature** from `(ctx, release, sourceID string)` to `(ctx, release, source *models.Source)` -- eliminates a redundant `GetSource` call since the source is already fetched in `Work`.

### `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker_test.go`

Added three new test functions:

- **`TestNotifyWorker_VersionFilterExclude`** -- verifies that a release matching the exclude pattern (`-(alpha|beta|rc)`) is filtered out: zero notifications sent, zero agent runs triggered.

- **`TestNotifyWorker_VersionFilterInclude`** -- verifies that a release not matching the include pattern (`^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$`) is filtered out: zero notifications sent.

- **`TestNotifyWorker_VersionFilterPassesThrough`** -- verifies that a release passing both include and exclude filters results in 1 notification being sent normally.

### Test Results

All 40 tests in `internal/routing` pass, including the 3 new tests and all pre-existing tests.

Three changes were made:
